\Omega^{2}\nabla^{i}\Delta_{g}\phi
.

Similarly, commuting (
6.17
) with
i
angular derivatives, we obtain

\begin{split}\Omega\nabla_{4}\nabla^{i}(\Omega\nabla_{A})\phi=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\bigg(\Omega e_{A}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)-\Omega^{2}{\chi_{A}}^{B}e_{B}\phi+\Omega e_{4}(\Omega)\nabla_{A}\phi\bigg)\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}(\Omega\nabla_{A})\phi\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\beta)\nabla^{i_{4}}(\Omega\nabla_{A})\phi.\end{split}

(6.28)

Notice that the term with the top-order derivative on the right is
\nabla^{i}[\Omega e_{A}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)]
.

We now consider

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{0}}^{u}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}u^{2i}\nabla_{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)[\Omega\nabla_{3}\nabla^{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)+\frac{i+1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)]\\
&+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{u_{0}}^{u}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}u^{2i}\nabla_{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}(\Omega\nabla^{A})\phi[\Omega\nabla_{4}\nabla^{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}(\Omega\nabla_{A})\phi].\end{split}

(6.29)

Employing (
6.27
) and (
6.28
), on the right we encounter

\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}\Omega^{2}u^{2i}\nabla_{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\nabla^{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}\Delta_{g}\phi+\Omega^{2}u^{2i}\nabla_{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla^{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}\nabla_{A}(\Omega e_{4}\phi).

Employing integration by parts with respect to
\nabla_{A}
, we then cancel the top-order derivatives
\nabla^{i+1}(\nabla\phi)
,
\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)
. Applying Proposition
6.2
for (
6.29
), together with (
6.27
) and (
6.28
) we arrive at

\begin{split}&\|u^{i}\nabla^{i}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|\Omega u^{i}\nabla^{i}(e_{A}\phi)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{i}\nabla^{i}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{-1,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{2i}\Omega^{2}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\Delta_{g}\phi\nabla^{i}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\big(-\frac{1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi\cdot{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}e_{3}\phi+2\Omega^{2}\eta^{A}e_{A}\phi\big)\nabla^{i}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|u^{2i}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i,\\
i_{4}<i\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\nabla^{i}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\underline{\chi}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\nabla^{i}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\underline{\beta})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\nabla^{i}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|\Omega^{2}u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\big(e_{A}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\big)\nabla^{i}\nabla_{A}\phi\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|\Omega^{2}u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla^{i}\nabla_{A}\phi\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|\Omega^{2}u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\beta)\nabla^{i_{4}}\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla^{i}\nabla_{A}\phi\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.\end{split}

(6.30)

We then bound each of the terms on the right. For the initial data term, we have

\|u^{i}\nabla^{i}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{-1,\underline{u}})}\leq(\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|)^{2}=\underline{u}a.

The terms with top-order derivatives (when
i=5
) can be controlled as follows: we first bound the terms involving
\Omega e_{3}\phi
and get

\begin{split}&\|u^{10}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)}S\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}S\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O=\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)}S^{2}O\cdot\underline{u}a\leq\underline{u}aB^{-\frac{1}{4}},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|u^{10}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{-1}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a\tilde{O}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}S\cdot\frac{1}{|u|}=\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{O}S}{|u|}\underline{u}a\leq\underline{u}aB^{-\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

For the top-order terms with
\eta
, we have

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{2}u^{10}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{2}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)}S\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}|u|O=\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|}\underline{u}a{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)}S^{2}O\leq\underline{u}aB^{-1},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{2}u^{10}e_{A}\phi\nabla^{5}\eta\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{2}e_{A}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\Omega^{-1}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{O}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}O=\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega}\tilde{O}SO\cdot\underline{u}a\leq\underline{u}aB^{-\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

For the top-order terms containing
\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0}
, they can be controlled via

\begin{split}&\|u^{10}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0})\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}S\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}O\leq\underline{u}aB^{-\frac{1}{2}},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|u^{10}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0})\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega e_{4}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)}\tilde{O}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}S\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O=\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\tilde{O}SO}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\underline{u}a{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)}\leq\underline{u}aB^{-\frac{1}{4}}.\end{split}

The top-order terms involving
\Omega\chi
obey

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{2}u^{10}(\Omega\chi)\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|\Omega u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega\chi\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}S\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}S\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\underline{u}O\leq\underline{u}aB^{-\frac{1}{2}},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|u^{10}\Omega^{2}e_{A}\phi\nabla^{5}(\Omega\chi)\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{10}\Omega^{2}e_{A}\phi\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\chi})\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{10}\Omega^{2}e_{A}\phi\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\chi})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega e_{A}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega u^{-1}e_{A}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{O}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O+\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a\tilde{O}^{\prime}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O\\
=&\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\tilde{O}SO\cdot\underline{u}a+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{5}{2}}a\tilde{O}^{\prime}SO}{|u|^{\frac{5}{2}}}\underline{u}a\leq\underline{u}aB^{-1}.\end{split}

Finally, the top-order terms with
\beta
and
\underline{\beta}
can be bounded via

\begin{split}&\|u^{10}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\nabla^{4}(\Omega\underline{\beta})\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|\Omega^{-1}u^{6}\nabla^{4}(\Omega\underline{\beta})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega u^{-1}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)}\mathcal{R}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}S\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O=\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\mathcal{R}SO}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)}\underline{u}a\leq\underline{u}aB^{-1},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{2}u^{10}e_{A}\phi\nabla^{4}(\Omega\beta)\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{4}(\Omega\beta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega e_{A}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O=\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}SO}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\underline{u}a\leq\underline{u}aB^{-1}.\end{split}

It is a straight-forward check that the non-top-order-derivative terms in (
6.30
) obey the same (and even smaller) upper bounds, compared with the estimates above. There is no borderline term and their upper bounds are much smaller than
\underline{u}a
. Putting all the estimates together, we then finish the proof of this current proposition.
∎

Next, we move to establish the energy estimate for the other pair and we have

Proposition 6.6
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.38
), (
2.39
), (
2.40
), (
2.41
), it holds

\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}.

In addition, we also have the improved estimate

\begin{split}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}+(\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega^{\frac{3}{2}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}.\end{split}

Proof.

Employing the paired equations (
6.19
), (
6.20
)

\begin{split}\nabla_{\Omega e_{3}}\nabla_{A}\phi+\frac{1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla_{A}\phi=&e_{A}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-\Omega{\hat{\underline{\chi}}_{A}\,}^{B}\nabla_{B}\phi,\\
\nabla_{\Omega e_{4}}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]=&\Omega^{2}\Delta\phi-\frac{1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\cdot\Omega e_{4}\phi-\frac{1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi\cdot\Omega e_{3}\phi+2\Omega^{2}\underline{\eta}^{A}\cdot e_{A}\phi.\end{split}

and commutating them with
i
angular derivatives, for
i\geq 1
we arrive at

\begin{split}&(\Omega\nabla_{3})\nabla^{i}(e_{A}\phi)+\frac{i+1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i}(e_{A}\phi)\\
=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\bigg(\nabla_{A}(\Omega\nabla_{3}\phi)-\Omega{\hat{\underline{\chi}}_{A}\,}^{B}\nabla_{B}\phi\bigg)\\
&+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i,\\
i_{4}<i\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}(e_{A}\phi)\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0})\nabla^{i_{4}}(e_{A}\phi)\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\underline{\beta})\nabla^{i_{4}}(e_{A}\phi),\end{split}

\begin{split}&(\Omega\nabla_{4})\nabla^{i}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\\
=&(\Omega\nabla_{4})\nabla^{i}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]\\
=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\bigg(\Omega^{2}\Delta_{g}\phi-\frac{1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\cdot\Omega e_{4}\phi-\frac{1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi\cdot\Omega e_{3}\phi+2\Omega^{2}\underline{\eta}^{A}e_{A}\phi\bigg)\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\beta)\nabla^{i_{4}}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}].\end{split}

With
i\geq 1
, we then consider

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{-1}^{u}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}u^{2i}\nabla_{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}(e^{A}\phi)[\Omega\nabla_{3}\nabla^{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}(e_{A}\phi)+\frac{i+1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}(e_{A}\phi)]\\
&+\int_{0}^{\underline{u}}\int_{-1}^{u}\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}u^{2i}\nabla_{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]\{\Omega^{-2}\cdot\Omega\nabla_{4}\nabla^{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]\}.\end{split}

Using (
6.19
) and (
6.20
) into the above expression, we see that the terms involving the top-order derivatives are of the form

\int_{S_{u,\underline{u}}}u^{2i}\nabla_{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}(e^{A}\phi)\nabla^{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}[e_{A}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)]+u^{2i}\Omega^{-2}\Omega^{2}\nabla_{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\nabla^{A_{1}\cdot\cdot\cdot A_{i}}(\Delta_{g}\phi).

Employing integration by parts with respect to
\nabla_{A}
, we notice that the top-order-derivative terms
\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)
and
\nabla^{i+1}(e_{A}\phi)
are
cancelled
. Carrying out the details of this approach, we get

\begin{split}&\|u^{i}\nabla^{i}(e_{A}\phi)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|\Omega^{-1}u^{i}\nabla^{i}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{i}\nabla^{i}(e_{A}\phi)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{-1,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\nabla_{A}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\nabla^{i}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega{\hat{\underline{\chi}}_{A}\,}^{B}\nabla_{B}\phi)\nabla^{i}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i,\,i_{4}<i}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i_{4}}(e_{A}\phi)\nabla^{i}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0})\nabla^{i_{4}}(e_{A}\phi)\nabla^{i}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\underline{\beta})\nabla^{i_{4}}(e_{A}\phi)\nabla^{i}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|\Omega^{-2}\Omega^{2}u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\Delta_{g}\phi\nabla^{i}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|\Omega^{-2}u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\nabla^{i_{3}}\bigg(-\frac{1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\cdot\Omega e_{4}\phi-\frac{1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi\cdot\Omega e_{3}\phi+2\Omega^{2}\underline{\eta}^{A}e_{A}\phi\bigg)\nabla^{i}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\end{split}

\begin{split}&+\|\Omega^{-2}u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\chi)\nabla^{i_{4}}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]\nabla^{i}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|\Omega^{-2}u^{2i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}(\eta+\underline{\eta})^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\beta)\nabla^{i_{4}}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]\nabla^{i}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.\end{split}

(6.31)

We now derive bound for terms on the right. The initial-data term obeys

\|u^{i}\nabla^{i}(e_{A}\phi)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{-1,\underline{u}})}\leq(\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|)^{2}=(\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|})^{2}=(\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}})^{2}\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{2}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}.

The terms with top-order derivatives (when
i=5
) are controlled as below: we first bound the 4 terms involving
\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)
and they satisfy

\begin{split}&\|u^{10}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi))\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega(\eta,\underline{\eta})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}O\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|u^{10}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0})\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}O\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot(\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega^{\frac{3}{2}}})^{\frac{1}{2}},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|u^{10}(e_{A}\phi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|e_{A}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\tilde{O}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|u^{10}(e_{A}\phi)\nabla^{4}(\Omega\underline{\beta})\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{6}\Omega^{-1}\nabla^{4}(\Omega\underline{\beta})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{-1}\Omega e_{A}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\mathcal{R}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{3}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega}.\end{split}

For top-order-derivative terms containing
\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)
and
\Omega\chi
, we have

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{-2}u^{10}(\Omega\chi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega\chi\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\underline{u}O\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot(\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega})^{\frac{1}{2}},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{-2}u^{10}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{-1}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a\tilde{O}^{\prime}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}S\cdot\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\cdot\frac{1}{|u|^{2}\Omega}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega}.\end{split}

Noting that in above inequality, we employ the crucial estimates via
\tilde{O}^{\prime}
.

To deal with the top-order-derivative terms involving both
\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)
and
\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}
, we encounter the borderline estimate. Using the derived Proposition
6.5
, we have

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{-2}u^{10}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{|u|\Omega}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&c_{0}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\frac{1}{c_{0}}(\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega})^{2}\cdot\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

By choosing
c_{0}
to be suitably small, the term
c_{0}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
can be absorbed to the left of (
6.31
).

The other top-order-derivative terms with
\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}
satisfies a better upper bound

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{-2}u^{10}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\tilde{O}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega}\underline{u}O\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot(\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega^{\frac{3}{2}}})^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{1}{2}}}.\end{split}

For the top-order-derivative terms containing
\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)
and
\underline{\eta}
, we have

\begin{split}&\|u^{10}\underline{\eta}^{A}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega\underline{\eta}^{A}\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|u^{10}(e_{A}\phi)\nabla^{5}\underline{\eta}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\underline{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega e_{A}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\tilde{O}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega}.\end{split}

The top-order-derivative terms involving
\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)
and
\Omega e_{3}\phi-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}
obey

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{-2}u^{10}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\|\Omega^{-1}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{O}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}\Omega}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{-2}u^{10}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\chi})\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\chi})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{O}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}\Omega}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega}.\end{split}

And the top-order-derivative term with
\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)
and
\nabla^{4}(\Omega\beta)
obey

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{-2}u^{10}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]\nabla^{4}(\Omega\beta)\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{4}(\Omega\beta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\|\Omega^{-1}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}\Omega}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega}.\end{split}

We hence bound all the top-order-derivative terms. It is a straight-forward check that compared with the upper bounds of the obtained estimates above, the rest terms obey the same (and even smaller) upper bounds. There is no other borderline term and their upper bounds are much smaller than
\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}
. Gathering all these estimates, we finish the proof of this current proposition.

∎

7.
Elliptic estimates for the fifth derivatives of the Ricci coefficients

We now move to bound the fifth angular derivatives for the Ricci coefficients. We start from the estimates for
\hat{\omega}
.

Proposition 7.1
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.38
), (
2.39
), (
2.40
), (
2.41
), it holds

\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\omega})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}[1+\mathcal{R}(\beta)].

Proof.

We first construct an auxiliary function
\omega^{\dagger}
, which satisfies

\nabla_{3}\omega^{\dagger}=\frac{1}{2}\check{\sigma}

and has zero initial data on
H_{-1}
. We further let

\kappa:=\nabla\hat{\omega}+^{*}\nabla\omega^{\dagger}-\frac{1}{2}(\beta-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla\phi).

Using Proposition
3.6
and bootstrap assumption (
2.39
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i}\nabla^{i}\omega^{\dagger}\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq

\displaystyle\|\Omega u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}\check{\sigma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{-1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq a^{\frac{1}{2}}B^{-\frac{1}{4}}.

This also implies

\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i}\nabla^{i}(\Omega\omega^{\dagger})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq a^{\frac{1}{2}}B^{-\frac{1}{4}}\leq a^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Note that in above inequality
\Omega\omega^{\dagger}
obeys the same bounds as for
\psi\hskip 1.0pt
. Hence we regard
\Omega\omega^{\dagger}
as one of the
\psi\hskip 1.0pt
terms and we employ the notation
\psi\hskip 1.0pt\in\{\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi,\Omega\hat{\chi},\Omega\hat{\omega},\Omega\omega^{\dagger}\}
. Using this notation, we write the transport equation for
\Omega\kappa
as

\begin{split}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\nabla_{3}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\kappa)+\frac{1}{2}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\kappa)\\
=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=1}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{1}}\nabla^{i_{2}}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{|u|},\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})\cdot\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=1}\Omega^{2}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{1}+1}\nabla^{i_{2}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},e_{A}\phi)\\
&+\frac{1}{|u|}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\psi\hskip 1.0pt+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}}{|u|}\beta+\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0ptK+\nabla(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\psi\hskip 1.0pt+\frac{1}{|u|}\nabla(\Omega e_{4}\phi).\end{split}

Commuting with angular derivatives for
4
times and employing the schematic Codazzi equation
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\beta=\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=1}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{1}}\nabla^{i_{2}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt
, we obtain

\begin{split}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\nabla_{3}\nabla^{4}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\kappa)+\frac{5}{2}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{4}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\kappa)\\
=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=5}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{|u|},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\underline{\chi}})\cdot\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=5\\
i_{1}\leq 4\end{subarray}}\Omega^{2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\eta,\underline{\eta},e_{A}\phi)\\
&+\frac{1}{|u|}\nabla^{4}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\beta)+\frac{1}{|u|}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4\\
i_{1}\leq 3\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}+1}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt}\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=4}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{4}}K{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)+\frac{1}{|u|}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)}.\end{split}

Employing Proposition
3.6
with
\lambda_{0}=\frac{5}{2}
, we then bound the
\|u^{4}\nabla^{4}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\kappa)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L_{u}^{\infty}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
norm by the
\|u^{4}\cdot\|_{L_{\underline{u}}^{2}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
norm of the right.

In the first term, we encounter
u^{4}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})
and it satisfies

\begin{split}\|u^{4}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\|u^{-1}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\mathcal{R}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}O\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}B^{-\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|}.\end{split}

The rest of the first term obey

\begin{split}\lesssim&\|u^{4}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{|u|},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\underline{\chi}})\nabla^{5}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=5\\
i_{1},i_{3}\leq 4\end{subarray}}\|u^{4}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\|u^{2}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{-3}\|_{L^{1}_{u}}\\
&\quad\times\bigg(\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\chi,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\chi},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\omega})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L_{\underline{u}}^{2}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\omega^{\dagger})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L_{\underline{u}}^{2}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}aO^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\\
\lesssim&\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|^{2}}\cdot\bigg(\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{O}+\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\omega^{\dagger})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L_{\underline{u}}^{2}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}aO^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\\
\leq&\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}B^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}+\frac{B^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\omega^{\dagger})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L_{\underline{u}}^{2}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.\end{split}

In the second term, there is
\Omega^{2}u^{4}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\eta,\underline{\eta},e_{A}\phi)
and it obeys

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{2}u^{4}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\eta,\underline{\eta},e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\underline{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{2}u^{-1}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|\Omega u^{5}\nabla^{5}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega u^{-1}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|\Omega u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega u^{-1}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\cdot\tilde{O}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}O+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}(\tilde{O}+S)\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|^{3}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}B^{-1}}{|u|}.\end{split}

The rest of the second term can be bounded by

\begin{split}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=5\\
i_{1},i_{3}\leq 4\end{subarray}}\|\Omega^{2}u^{4}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}aO^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}B^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}.\end{split}

The third term containing
\beta
satisfies

\begin{split}\|u^{3}\nabla^{4}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\|u^{-2}\|_{L^{1}_{u}}\|u^{5}\nabla^{4}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\beta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{R}(\beta).\end{split}

For the next two terms, we have

\begin{split}&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4}\|u^{3}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}aO^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}B^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|},\end{split}

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4\\
i_{1}\leq 3\end{subarray}}\|u^{4}\nabla^{i_{1}+1}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}aO^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}B^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}.

The sixth term containing
K
obeys

\begin{split}&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4}\|u^{4}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}K\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O\bigg(\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}}{|u|^{3}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\mathcal{R}+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\bigg)\lesssim\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}B^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|},\end{split}

where we use Proposition
4.1
and the bootstrap assumption (
2.40
).

For the last two terms, we have

\begin{split}\|u^{4}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq&\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega u^{-1}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)S\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}B^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}.\end{split}

And by Proposition (
6.5
), it holds

\begin{split}\|u^{3}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{-2}\|_{L^{1}_{u}}\lesssim\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}.\end{split}

Collecting all the above estimates, we arrive at

\|u^{4}\nabla^{4}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\kappa)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}[1+\mathcal{R}(\beta)]+\frac{B^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\omega^{\dagger})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L_{\underline{u}}^{2}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.

This also implies

\|u^{5}\nabla^{4}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\kappa)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}[1+\mathcal{R}(\beta)+\frac{B^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\omega^{\dagger})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L_{\underline{u}}^{2}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}].

(7.1)

We then appeal to the following
div
-
curl
system:

\mbox{div }\nabla\hat{\omega}=\mbox{div }\kappa+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{div }(\beta-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla\phi),\quad\mbox{curl }\nabla\hat{\omega}=0,

\mbox{curl }\nabla\omega^{\dagger}=\mbox{curl }\kappa+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{curl }(\beta-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla\phi),\quad\mbox{div }\nabla\omega^{\dagger}=0.

Applying the elliptic estimates from Proposition
3.11
, we get

\begin{split}&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\omega},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\omega^{\dagger})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\sum_{j\leq 4}\bigg(\|u^{j+1}\nabla^{j}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\kappa)\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{j+1}\nabla^{j}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\beta-\frac{1}{2}\Omega\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla\phi)\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{j}\nabla^{j}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\omega},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\omega^{\dagger})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)\\
\lesssim&\|u^{5}\nabla^{4}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\kappa)\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{j\leq 4}\bigg(\|u^{j+1}\nabla^{j}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\beta-\frac{1}{2}\Omega\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla\phi)\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{j}\nabla^{j}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\omega},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\omega^{\dagger})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg).\end{split}

After taking
L^{2}
in
\underline{u}
, together with (
7.1
), the above inequality gives

\begin{split}&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\omega},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\omega^{\dagger})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg(1+\mathcal{R}(\beta)+\frac{B^{-\frac{1}{2}}}{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\omega^{\dagger})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L_{\underline{u}}^{2}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}[1+\mathcal{R}(\beta)].\end{split}

With
B
sufficiently large, we absorb the
u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\omega^{\dagger})
term to the left and get

\begin{split}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\omega},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\omega^{\dagger})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}[1+\mathcal{R}(\beta)].\end{split}

∎

We then derive the estimates for
\mbox{tr}\chi
and
\hat{\chi}
.

Proposition 7.2
.

With the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.38
), (
2.39
), (
2.40
),(
2.41
), we have

\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a[1+\mathcal{R}(\beta)],\quad\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}},

\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\chi})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}[1+\mathcal{R}(\beta)].

Proof.

We use the following equation:

(\Omega\nabla_{4})(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)=-\frac{1}{2}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)^{2}-|\Omega\hat{\chi}|^{2}-4\Omega\hat{\omega}\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi-(\Omega\nabla_{4}\phi)^{2}.

Commuting it with
i
angular derivatives, we get

\begin{split}(\Omega\nabla_{4})\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)=&\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=5}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt.\end{split}

Via applying Proposition
3.5
, we can bound
\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L_{\underline{u}}^{\infty}L_{u}^{\infty}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
by the
\|u^{6}\cdot\|_{L_{u}^{\infty}L_{\underline{u}}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
norm of the right. Together with
\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)(u,0)=0
, we get

\begin{split}\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq&\|u^{6}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=5}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
+&\|u^{6}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=5\\
i_{3},i_{4}\leq 4\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\underline{u}a(O\tilde{O}+O^{2}).\end{split}

(7.2)

This gives

\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a(O\tilde{O}+O^{2})\ll\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a\tilde{O}^{\prime}.

(7.3)

It also implies

\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(7.4)

We then use the Codazzi equation with the schematic form

\mbox{div }({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\chi})-\frac{1}{2}\nabla({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\chi)+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\beta=\psi\hskip 1.0pt\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt.

Applying the elliptic estimates in Proposition
3.12
, we obtain

\begin{split}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\chi})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\sum_{i\leq 5}\|u^{i}\nabla^{i}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}(\Omega\beta)\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\sum_{i\leq 4}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=i}\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{2}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i}\nabla^{i}(\Omega\hat{\chi})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.\end{split}

We further take the
L^{2}
norm in
\underline{u}
. With the bound (
7.3
) for
\nabla^{5}\mbox{tr}\chi
, we deduce

\begin{split}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}&({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\chi})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\sum_{i\leq 5}\|u^{i}\nabla^{i}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}(\Omega\beta{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-\frac{\Omega}{2}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}(\Omega\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}}\\
&+\sum_{i\leq 4}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=i}\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{2}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i}\nabla^{i}(\Omega\hat{\chi})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}(\beta)+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}aO^{2}}{|u|}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}[1+\mathcal{R}(\beta)].\end{split}

With the above estimate for
u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\chi})
, together with Proposition
4.3
,
Proposition
5.1
, Proposition
6.5
, we then revisit (
7.2
) and obtain

\begin{split}\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}&(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi,\Omega\hat{\omega})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\omega})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u\Omega\hat{\chi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\chi})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u\Omega\nabla_{4}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\nabla_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\|u^{6}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=5\\
i_{3},i_{4}\leq 4\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{4}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a\tilde{O}+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega^{2}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}[1+\mathcal{R}(\beta)]+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}[1+\mathcal{R}(\beta)]{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}\\
\leq&\underline{u}a[1+\mathcal{R}(\beta)].\end{split}

This further implies

\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a[1+\mathcal{R}(\beta)].

(7.5)

∎

We then control the highest derivative of
\underline{\eta}
.

Proposition 7.3
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.38
), (
2.39
), (
2.40
), (
2.41
), it holds

\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\underline{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.

Proof.

We define
\underline{\mu}
via

\underline{\mu}=-\mbox{div }\underline{\eta}+K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi.

Thus
\underline{\eta}
and
\underline{\mu}
satisfy the following Hodge system:

\mbox{div }\underline{\eta}=-\underline{\mu}+K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi,\quad\mbox{curl }\underline{\eta}=-\check{\sigma}.

Recall
\nabla_{3}\underline{\eta}_{A}=-\underline{\chi}_{AB}\cdot(\underline{\eta}-\eta)_{B}+\underline{\beta}_{A}-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{A}\phi\cdot e_{3}\phi
and

\begin{split}\nabla_{3}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-&\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)+\frac{3}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)\\
=&\mbox{div }(\underline{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla\phi)-\frac{1}{2}\nabla^{A}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi-\zeta\cdot\underline{\beta}+2\eta\cdot\underline{\beta}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\underline{\chi}}\cdot\nabla\widehat{\otimes}\eta+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\underline{\chi}}\cdot(\eta\widehat{\otimes}\eta)-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}|\eta|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\eta^{A}\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla_{3}\phi\\
&+\frac{1}{2}(\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla_{B}\phi-\frac{1}{2}g_{AB}\nabla_{C}\phi\nabla^{C}\phi)\cdot\hat{\underline{\chi}}^{AB}-\frac{1}{8}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}(-\mbox{div }\eta+K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)-\frac{\Omega^{-1}}{|u|^{2}}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{|u|}).\end{split}

Hence, in the schematic form, we have that
\underline{\mu}
obeys

\begin{split}\Omega\nabla_{3}\underline{\mu}+\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\,\underline{\mu}=&\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\,\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\,\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt+\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})+\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\mbox{div }\eta+\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}K+\frac{\Omega}{|u|^{3}}\\
&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\,\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\,\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt+\Delta_{g}\phi(\Omega e_{3}\phi)+e_{A}\phi e^{A}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)+\eta e_{A}\phi(\Omega e_{3}\phi)}.\end{split}

Commuting with
4
angular derivatives, we get

\begin{split}&\Omega\nabla_{3}\nabla^{4}\underline{\mu}+3\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{4}\underline{\mu}\\
=&\frac{\Omega}{|u|}\nabla^{5}\eta+\Omega e_{3}\phi\cdot\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)+e_{A}\phi\cdot\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)+\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\eta,\underline{\eta},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})+\frac{1}{|u|}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\\
&+\frac{1}{|u|}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}K+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+2}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}K\\
&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4\\
i_{3}\leq 3\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}e_{A}\phi\nabla^{i_{4}}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4\\
i_{4}\leq 3\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}e_{A}\phi\nabla^{i_{4}+1}(\Omega e_{3}\phi).}\end{split}

We then employ Proposition
3.6
with
\lambda_{0}=3
and
\|u^{5}\nabla^{4}\underline{\mu}\|_{L_{u}^{\infty}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
can be controlled by the
\|u^{5}\cdot\|_{L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
norm of the right hand side. We bound each of these terms. We begin with terms containing the
5
angular derivatives on the Ricci coefficients. First note that by Proposition
7.4
, we have

\begin{split}&\|\Omega u^{4}\nabla^{5}\eta\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\|\Omega u^{-2}\|_{L^{1}_{u}}\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}}{|u|}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\mathcal{R}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.\end{split}

The next term is a borderline term. Via Proposition
6.5
, we control it as

\begin{split}&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|\Omega u^{5}\nabla^{5}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\Omega^{-1}}{|u|}\cdot|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}=\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.\end{split}

And the term containing
\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)
obeys

\begin{split}&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)(e_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}A}}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega e_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}A}}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\mathcal{S}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O=\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\mathcal{S}O\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.\end{split}

Then the top-order-derivative terms with
\nabla^{5}\eta
or
\nabla^{5}\underline{\eta}
obey

\begin{split}&\|u^{5}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{-2}\|_{L^{1}_{u}}\|u^{2}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\underline{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{-1}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\tilde{O}+\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}O\tilde{O}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.\end{split}

And the top-order-derivative terms
\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})
satisfy

\begin{split}&\|u^{5}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}O\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\tilde{O}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.\end{split}

The next term is lower-order and we have

\begin{split}&\|u^{5}\frac{1}{|u|}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{5}{2}}aO^{2}}{|u|^{2}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.\end{split}

For the term containing
K
, via using Proposition
3.4
, we deduce

\begin{split}&\|u^{5}\frac{1}{|u|}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}K\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.\end{split}

The remaining lower-order terms are all bounded by
\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}
. Hence, combining the above estimates, we arrive at

\|u^{5}\nabla^{4}\underline{\mu}\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.

By using the div-curl system

\mbox{div }\underline{\eta}=-\underline{\mu}+K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi,\quad\mbox{curl }\underline{\eta}=-\check{\sigma}

and employing elliptic estimates from Proposition
3.11
, we further deduce

\begin{split}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\underline{\eta}\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\|u^{5}\nabla^{4}\underline{\mu}\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{5}\nabla^{4}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi,\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\sum_{i\leq 3}\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}(-\underline{\mu}+K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi,\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}}{|u|}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\mathcal{R}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.\end{split}

∎

We proceed to obtain the highest order derivative estimates for
\eta
. In the proof we will derive an improved bound for
\nabla^{i}\mu
with
1\leq i\leq 4
.

Proposition 7.4
.

With the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.38
), (
2.39
), (
2.40
), (
2.41
) and the proved
\mathcal{O}_{5,2}(\underline{\eta})\lesssim 1
, we have

\begin{split}\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)[1+\mathcal{R}(K,\check{\sigma})]+\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot a^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mathcal{R}(\beta),\end{split}

\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-1}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}[1+\underline{\mathcal{R}}(K,\check{\sigma})],

\|u^{5}\Omega\nabla^{5}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}[1+\underline{\mathcal{R}}(K,\check{\sigma})].

And
\nabla^{i}\mu
obeys the below improved bound:

\sum_{1\leq i\leq 4}\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}\mu\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{u}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.

Proof.

We first define the mass aspect function
\mu

\mu:=-\mbox{div }\eta+K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi.

Recall that
\eta
obeys the following elliptic system

\mbox{div }\eta=-\mu+K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi,\quad\quad\mbox{curl }\eta=\check{\sigma}.

And via employing the below renormalized null Bianchi equation

\begin{split}&\nabla_{4}[\Omega(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)]+\mbox{tr}\chi[\Omega(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)]\\
=&-\Omega\mbox{div }(\beta-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\nabla\phi)-\frac{1}{2}\Omega\nabla^{A}\nabla_{4}\phi\cdot\nabla_{A}\phi-2\Omega\hat{\omega}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)-\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi\cdot\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\\
&+\Omega[-\zeta\cdot\beta-2\underline{\eta}\cdot\beta+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}\cdot\nabla\widehat{\otimes}\underline{\eta}+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}\cdot(\underline{\eta}\widehat{\otimes}\underline{\eta})-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\mbox{div }\underline{\eta}-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi|\underline{\eta}|^{2}]\\
&+\Omega[\frac{1}{2}\underline{\eta}^{A}\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla_{4}\phi+\frac{1}{2}(\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla_{B}\phi-\frac{1}{2}g_{AB}\nabla_{C}\phi\nabla^{C}\phi)\cdot\hat{\chi}^{AB}],\end{split}

(7.6)

we deduce that
\mu
satisfies the schematic equation

\begin{split}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\nabla_{4})\mu=&\chi\nabla(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\psi\hskip 1.0pt\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\hskip 1.0pt\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt+\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\chi,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\chi})+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\psi\hskip 1.0pt(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)+\frac{\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi}{|u|^{2}}}\\
&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\Delta_{g}\phi({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}e_{4}\phi)+\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla^{A}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}e_{4}\phi}).\end{split}

Commuting with
i
angular derivatives, we have

\begin{split}&({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\nabla_{4})\nabla^{i}\mu\\
=&\Omega e_{4}\phi\cdot\nabla^{i+1}(e_{A}\phi)+e_{A}\phi\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)+\chi\nabla^{i+1}(\eta,\underline{\eta})+\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i+1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\chi,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\chi}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\nabla^{4}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)}\\
&+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i+1\\
i_{1},i_{3}\leq i\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{4}}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)}\\
&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i\\
i_{3}\leq i-1\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}(\nabla_{A}\phi)\nabla^{i_{4}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}e_{4}\phi)}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i\\
i_{3}\leq i-1\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}+1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}e_{4}\phi)\nabla^{i_{4}}(e_{A}\phi)}.\\
\end{split}

Since
\nabla^{i}\mu
vanishes on
\underline{u}=0
, by Proposition
3.5
, to bound
\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}\mu\|_{L_{\underline{u}}^{\infty}L_{u}^{\infty}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
, we only need to control the
\|u^{i+2}\cdot\|_{L_{u}^{\infty}L_{\underline{u}}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
norm of the right. We now estimate them.

Via employing the obtained estimates in Proposition (
6.5
), Proposition (
6.6
) and Proposition (
5.1
), the terms involving the highest angular derivatives of the scalar field satisfy

\begin{split}&\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i+1}(e_{A}\phi)(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i+1}(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\bigg(\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}+(\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega^{\frac{3}{2}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\bigg)\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)(e_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|ue_{A}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{S}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O=\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\mathcal{S}O\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.\end{split}

The terms containing the highest angular derivatives of
\eta
and
\underline{\eta}
obey

\begin{split}&\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i+2}\chi\nabla^{i+1}(\eta,\underline{\eta})\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\chi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}\eta\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u\chi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\underline{\eta}\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|}\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\mathcal{R}+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O(\chi)\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\tilde{O}(\underline{\eta})\lesssim\underline{u}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.\end{split}

For the last line we utilize Proposition (
4.2
), Proposition (
4.3
) and Proposition (
7.3
).

The terms with the highest derivatives of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\chi
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\chi}
satisfy

\begin{split}&\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i+2}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i+1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\chi,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\chi})\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\|u^{2}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\bigg(\sum_{i\leq 3}\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i+1}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\chi,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\chi})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\chi,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\hat{\chi})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)\\
\lesssim&\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{O}\lesssim\frac{\underline{u}^{2}aO\tilde{O}}{|u|}\lesssim\underline{u}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.\end{split}

By Proposition (
4.3
), we also have

\begin{split}\|u^{4}\nabla^{4}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq&\|\frac{1}{|u|}\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{4}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi-\Omega^{2}(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\chi)_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{3}\nabla^{4}\Omega^{2}\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\frac{\underline{u}}{|u|}\cdot\underline{u}a+\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\cdot\underline{u}|u|O\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}+\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}O}{|u|}\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.\end{split}

Employing Proposition
4.1
together with the bootstrap assumption (
2.39
), we then bound the other lower order terms and they obey
\lesssim\underline{u}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}
.

Combining all the estimates above, we obtain

\begin{split}\sum_{1\leq i\leq 4}\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}\mu\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\underline{u}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}.\end{split}

(7.7)

We then recall the div-curl system

\mbox{div }\eta=-\mu+K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi,\quad\mbox{curl }\eta=\check{\sigma}.

Via employing elliptic estimates from Proposition
3.11
and the fact
\Omega\leq 1
, we obtain

\begin{split}&\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}\eta\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
