\lesssim&\sum_{i\leq 4}\bigg(\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}\mu\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi,\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}\eta\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)\\
\lesssim&\underline{u}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\cdot\mathcal{R}(K)+\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi,\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}(\beta),\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|u^{5}\Omega\nabla^{5}\eta\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\sum_{i\leq 4}\bigg(\|\Omega u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}\mu\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|\Omega u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi,\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|\Omega u^{i}\nabla^{i}\eta\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)\\
\lesssim&\sum_{i\leq 4}\|\Omega u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi,\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{3}{4}}}{|u|}\cdot\Omega\cdot\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\cdot\mathcal{R}(K)+\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\cdot\Omega O.\end{split}

We then take the
L^{2}
norm in
\underline{u}
and
u
respectively. Via using (
7.7
), we further obtain

\begin{split}\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\cdot\mathcal{R}(K,\check{\sigma})+\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot a^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mathcal{R}(\beta),\end{split}

\begin{split}\|\Omega u^{5}\nabla^{5}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{\mathcal{R}}(K,\check{\sigma})+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}[1+\underline{\mathcal{R}}(K,\check{\sigma})].\end{split}

(7.8)

This also infers

\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\Omega^{-1}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot[1+\underline{\mathcal{R}}(K,\check{\sigma})].

We hence finish the proof of this proposition.
∎

Finally, we move to bound the highest order estimates for
\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}
and
\hat{\underline{\chi}}
.

Proposition 7.5
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.38
), (
2.39
), (
2.40
), (
2.41
), it holds

\begin{split}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}\bigg(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\bigg),\\
\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bigg(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\bigg)},\\
\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\hat{\underline{\chi}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bigg(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\bigg)}\cdot\mathcal{R}(\beta).\end{split}

Proof.

We utilize the following equation for
\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}
:

\Omega\nabla_{3}(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})+\frac{1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})=-|\hat{\underline{\chi}}|^{2}-\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla_{3}\phi.

Via commuting the above equation with
5
angular derivatives, we obtain

\begin{split}&\Omega\nabla_{3}\nabla^{5}(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})+3\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{5}(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\\
=&\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega^{-1}\hat{\underline{\chi}})+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-1}}e_{3}\phi\nabla^{5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}e_{3}\phi)+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=5\\
i_{1},i_{3}\leq 4\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-2}}}{|u|}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=4}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-2}}}{|u|^{2}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}.\end{split}

(7.9)

In the derivation of above equation, we employ

\begin{split}e_{3}\phi\nabla^{5}(e_{3}\phi)=&e_{3}\phi\nabla^{5}[e_{3}\phi-(e_{3}\phi)_{0}]=e_{3}\phi\nabla^{5}[\Omega^{-1}\Omega(e_{3}\phi-(e_{3}\phi)_{0})]\\
=&\Omega^{-1}e_{3}\phi\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}=5\\
i_{2}\leq 4\end{subarray}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{1}}\nabla^{i_{2}}[\Omega(e_{3}\phi-(e_{3}\phi)_{0})]+\Omega^{-1}e_{3}\phi\nabla^{5}[\Omega(e_{3}\phi-(e_{3}\phi)_{0})]\\
=&\Omega^{-1}e_{3}\phi\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}=5\\
i_{2}\leq 4\end{subarray}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{1}}\nabla^{i_{2}}[\Omega(e_{3}\phi-(e_{3}\phi)_{0}]+\Omega^{-1}e_{3}\phi\nabla^{5}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]\\
&-\Omega^{-1}e_{3}\phi\nabla^{5}[(\Omega-\Omega_{0})(e_{3}\phi)_{0}].\end{split}

Note that
\Omega^{-1}e_{3}\phi\nabla^{5}[(\Omega e_{3}\phi)-(\Omega e_{3}\phi)_{0}]
is the same as the last term in (
7.9
) and the rest terms are absorbed by the other same-type terms in (
7.9
).

We also notice

\begin{split}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\|\Omega^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|},\\
\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\|\Omega\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|\Omega}.\end{split}

To bound
\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
, we then apply Proposition
3.6
with
\lambda_{0}=3
and it can be controlled by the
\|u^{5}\cdot\|_{L_{\underline{u}}^{\infty}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
norm of the right hand side of (
7.9
).

Together, to estimate
\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
, we first bound

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\|u^{5}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega^{-1}\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L_{\underline{u}}^{\infty}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\|\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\tilde{O}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}O=\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\tilde{O}O\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega^{\frac{1}{2}}}.\end{split}

To bound
\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
, similarly, we get

\begin{split}&\|\Omega\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\|u^{5}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega^{-1}\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L_{\underline{u}}^{\infty}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\|\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\tilde{O}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}O=\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\tilde{O}O\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega^{\frac{1}{2}}}.\end{split}

We then move to control the remaining terms. We first bound

\begin{split}&\|(\Omega^{2},\Omega)\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}e_{3}\phi\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\|(\Omega,1)\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega e_{3}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\|(\Omega,1)\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\|u^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{u}}\cdot\bigg(\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}+(\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega^{\frac{3}{2}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\bigg)\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\bigg(\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}+(\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega^{\frac{3}{2}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\bigg).\end{split}

Here for the third line, we employ Proposition
6.6
. For the rest terms, we have

\begin{split}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\|(\Omega^{2},\Omega)\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}}\cdot\|\Omega^{-2}u^{5}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=5\\
i_{1},i_{3}\leq 4\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L_{\underline{u}}^{\infty}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega^{\frac{1}{2}}},\end{split}

\begin{split}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\|(\Omega^{2},\Omega)\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}}\cdot\|u^{5}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=4}\frac{\Omega^{-2}}{|u|}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L_{\underline{u}}^{\infty}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega^{\frac{1}{2}}},\end{split}

\begin{split}&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\|(\Omega^{2},\Omega)\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}}\cdot\|u^{5}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=4}\frac{\Omega^{-2}}{|u|^{2}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\|_{L_{\underline{u}}^{\infty}L_{u}^{1}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot(\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega^{\frac{1}{2}}})^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

Collecting all the above estimates, we derive

\begin{split}&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot\Omega\cdot{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega})}+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega^{\frac{1}{2}}})^{\frac{1}{2}},\\
&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega})},\\
&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}+(\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega^{\frac{3}{2}}})^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega^{\frac{1}{2}}})^{\frac{1}{2}},\\
&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega})}.\end{split}

(7.10)

To bound the fifth angular derivatives of
\hat{\underline{\chi}}
, we now employ the Codazzi equation

\mbox{div }\hat{\underline{\chi}}=\underline{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-\frac{1}{2}(\eta-\underline{\eta})\cdot(\hat{\underline{\chi}}-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}).

Applying Proposition
3.12
, we get

\begin{split}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\sum_{i\leq 4}\bigg(\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}(\Omega\underline{\beta})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)+\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|}\\
\lesssim&\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\Omega}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega})+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot(\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega^{\frac{1}{2}}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}(\Omega\underline{\beta})\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|},\end{split}

\begin{split}\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\hat{\underline{\chi}}\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\sum_{i\leq 4}\bigg(\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i+1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}\underline{\beta}\|_{L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)+\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O(\eta,\underline{\eta})}{|u|\Omega}.\end{split}

We then take the
L^{2}
norm in
u
and obtain

\begin{split}&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)+\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}\underline{\beta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{-1}\Omega\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\\
\lesssim&{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega})}+\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\mathcal{R}\cdot\frac{1}{|u|}+\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega})},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\hat{\underline{\chi}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\sum_{i\leq 4}\bigg(\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i+1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}\underline{\beta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)+\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O(\eta,\underline{\eta})}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\\
\lesssim&\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i+1}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}\underline{\beta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\|u^{-1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}(\beta)}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\\
\lesssim&{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega})}+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\cdot\mathcal{R}(\beta),\end{split}

where we use Proposition
4.5
and Proposition
7.1
.

Collecting all above estimates, we hence prove this proposition.

∎

We conclude this section by summarizing all the proved estimates in this section with the aid of the
\tilde{\mathcal{O}}_{5,2}
norm:

Proposition 7.6
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.38
), (
2.39
), (
2.40
), (
2.41
), it holds

\tilde{\mathcal{O}}_{5,2}\lesssim 1+\mathcal{R}.

8.
Energy Estimates for Curvature Components

To carry out the energy estimates, we employ the below paired equations with suitable
\Omega
weights. They are from the renormalized null Bianchi equations. The first pair are

\begin{split}&\nabla_{3}[\Omega(\beta_{\cdot}-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla_{\cdot}\phi)]_{A}+\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\Omega(\beta_{A}-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)\\
=&-\Omega\nabla(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{C}\phi\nabla_{C}\phi)+\Omega{{}^{*}\nabla}\check{\sigma}-\frac{1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi+\Omega\underline{\omega}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi\\
&+\Omega[2\hat{\chi}\cdot\underline{\beta}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-3(\eta K\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-^{*}\eta\check{\sigma})+\frac{1}{2}(\nabla(\hat{\chi}\cdot\hat{\underline{\chi}})-^{*}\nabla(\hat{\chi}\wedge\hat{\underline{\chi}}))-\frac{3}{4}\eta\mbox{tr}\chi\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}]\\
&+\Omega[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{3}{2}}(\eta\hat{\chi}\cdot\hat{\underline{\chi}}-^{*}\eta\hat{\chi}\wedge\hat{\underline{\chi}})-\frac{1}{4}(\nabla\mbox{tr}\chi\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\mbox{tr}\chi\nabla\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})]\\
&+\frac{3}{4}\Omega\nabla_{A}(\nabla^{C}\phi\nabla_{C}\phi)-\Omega\nabla^{B}R_{BA}+\frac{1}{2}\eta_{A}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla_{4}\phi+\eta_{A}\nabla_{C}\phi\nabla^{C}\phi,\\
\end{split}

(8.1)

\begin{split}\nabla_{4}(\Omega\check{\sigma})+\frac{3}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\Omega\check{\sigma}=&-\Omega\mbox{div }^{*}(\beta-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla\phi)+\Omega(-\nabla_{1}R_{42}+\nabla_{2}R_{41})-2\Omega\hat{\omega}\check{\sigma}\\
&+\Omega[-\zeta\cdot^{*}\beta-2\underline{\eta}\cdot^{*}\beta+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}\cdot^{*}(\nabla\widehat{\otimes}\underline{\eta})+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}\cdot^{*}(\underline{\eta}\widehat{\otimes}\underline{\eta})]\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\Omega(\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla_{B}\phi-\frac{1}{2}g_{AB}\nabla_{C}\phi\nabla^{C}\phi)\wedge\hat{\chi}^{AB},\\
\end{split}

(8.2)

\begin{split}&\nabla_{4}[\Omega(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)]+\mbox{tr}\chi\Omega(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)\\
=&-\Omega\mbox{div }(\beta-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\nabla\phi)-\frac{1}{2}\Omega\nabla^{A}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi-2\Omega\hat{\omega}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)-\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi\cdot\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\\
&+\Omega[-\zeta\cdot\beta-2\underline{\eta}\cdot\beta+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}\cdot\nabla\widehat{\otimes}\underline{\eta}+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}\cdot(\underline{\eta}\widehat{\otimes}\underline{\eta})-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\mbox{div }\underline{\eta}-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi|\underline{\eta}|^{2}]\\
&+\Omega[\frac{1}{2}\underline{\eta}^{A}\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla_{4}\phi+\frac{1}{2}(\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla_{B}\phi-\frac{1}{2}g_{AB}\nabla_{C}\phi\nabla^{C}\phi)\cdot\hat{\chi}^{AB}].\end{split}

(8.3)

The second pair are

\begin{split}\nabla_{3}\check{\sigma}+\frac{3}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\check{\sigma}=&-\mbox{div }^{*}(\underline{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla\phi)-\zeta\cdot^{*}\underline{\beta}-2\eta\cdot^{*}\underline{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\underline{\chi}}\cdot^{*}(\nabla\widehat{\otimes}\eta)+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\underline{\chi}}\cdot^{*}(\eta\widehat{\otimes}\eta)\\
&+\nabla_{1}R_{32}-\nabla_{2}R_{31}+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\underline{\chi}}^{AB}\wedge(\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla_{B}\phi-\frac{1}{2}g_{AB}\nabla_{C}\phi\nabla^{C}\phi),\\
\end{split}

(8.4)

\begin{split}\nabla_{3}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-&\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)+\frac{3}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)\\
=&\mbox{div }(\underline{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla\phi)-\frac{1}{2}\nabla^{A}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi-\zeta\cdot\underline{\beta}+2\eta\cdot\underline{\beta}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\underline{\chi}}\cdot\nabla\widehat{\otimes}\eta+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\underline{\chi}}\cdot(\eta\widehat{\otimes}\eta)-\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}|\eta|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\eta^{A}\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla_{3}\phi\\
&+\frac{1}{2}(\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla_{B}\phi-\frac{1}{2}g_{AB}\nabla_{C}\phi\nabla^{C}\phi)\cdot\hat{\underline{\chi}}^{AB}-\frac{1}{8}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}(-\mbox{div }\eta+K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)-\frac{\Omega^{-1}}{|u|^{2}}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}+\frac{2}{|u|}),\end{split}

(8.5)

\begin{split}&\nabla_{4}(\underline{\beta}_{\cdot}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla_{\cdot}\phi)_{A}+\mbox{tr}\chi(\underline{\beta}_{A}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi})\\
=&\nabla(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)+^{*}\nabla\check{\sigma}+2\hat{\omega}\underline{\beta}+2\hat{\underline{\chi}}\cdot\beta+3(\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-\underline{\eta}K+^{*}\underline{\eta}\check{\sigma})\\
&+\nabla(-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi+\frac{1}{4}\mbox{tr}\chi\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-\frac{1}{2}\hat{\chi}\cdot\hat{\underline{\chi}})-\frac{1}{2}{{}^{*}\nabla}(\hat{\chi}\wedge\hat{\underline{\chi}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\frac{1}{2}\mbox{tr}\chi\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi}\\
&+\nabla_{A}(g^{CD}R_{CD})+\nabla^{B}R_{BA}-\frac{1}{2}\underline{\eta}_{A}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla_{4}\phi-\underline{\eta}_{A}\nabla_{C}\phi\nabla^{C}\phi.\\
\end{split}

(8.6)

Via applying Propositions
6.3
-
6.4
, for the first pair we have

Proposition 8.1
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.38
), (
2.39
), (
2.40
), (
2.41
), it holds

\begin{split}&\sum_{i\leq 4}(\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}(\Omega\beta-\frac{1}{2}\Omega\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|\Omega u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi,\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})})\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

By the obtained
L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
estimates in Section
5.1
for
\phi
, it also holds

\begin{split}&\sum_{i\leq 4}(\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}(\Omega\beta)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|\Omega u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}},\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})})\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

Proof.

Commuting equations for

\nabla_{3}[\Omega(\beta-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla\phi)],\quad\nabla_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}4}}(\Omega\check{\sigma}),\quad\nabla_{4}[\Omega(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)]

with
i
angular derivatives, we get

\nabla_{3}\nabla^{i}[\Omega(\beta_{\cdot}-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla_{\cdot}\phi]_{A}+\Omega\nabla\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{B}\phi\nabla_{B}\phi)-\Omega{{}^{*}\nabla}\nabla^{i}\check{\sigma}+\frac{2+i}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i}[\Omega(\beta_{\cdot}-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla_{\cdot}\phi]_{A}=G_{1,i}

(8.7)

with
G_{1,i}
being

\begin{split}G_{1,i}=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i+1}\psi\hskip 1.0pt+\frac{1}{|u|}\nabla^{i+1}\mbox{tr}\chi+\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i+1}(\hat{\underline{\chi}},\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}},\check{\sigma})\\
+&\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i+1\\
i_{1},i_{3}\leq i\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-1}}}{|u|}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}=i+1\\
i_{1}\leq i\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+2}\\
+&\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\Omega\nabla^{i+2}\phi\nabla\phi,}\end{split}

\nabla_{4}\nabla^{i}(\Omega\check{\sigma})+\Omega\mbox{div }^{*}\nabla^{i}(\beta-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla\phi)=G_{2,i},

(8.8)

where
G_{2,i}
is

\begin{split}G_{2,i}=&\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i+1}\psi\hskip 1.0pt+\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i+1}\underline{\eta}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{4}}\check{\sigma}\\
&+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i+1\\
i_{1},i_{3}\leq i\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\nabla\phi},\end{split}

\nabla_{4}\nabla^{i}[\Omega(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)]+\Omega\mbox{div }\nabla^{i}(\beta-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla\phi)=G_{3,i}

(8.9)

with
G_{3,i}
satisfying

\begin{split}G_{3,i}=&\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i+1}\psi\hskip 1.0pt+\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i+1}\underline{\eta}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{4}}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}},\check{\sigma})\\
&+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i+1\\
i_{1},i_{3}\leq i\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt+\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt+\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\nabla\phi.\end{split}

Note that by the structure of the renormalized null Bianchi equation, we will have
-\nabla_{1}R_{42}+\nabla_{2}R_{41}
, which enables us to avoid the troublesome term
\nabla^{i+2}\phi e_{4}\phi
in above two equations.

We now employ Proposition
6.2
for
\phi=u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}[\Omega(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)]
and
\phi=u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}[\Omega\check{\sigma}]
, and apply Proposition
6.4
for
\phi=\nabla^{i}[\Omega(\beta-\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{4}\phi\nabla\phi)]
with
\lambda_{1}=i+1
. We then add these identities together and obtain

\begin{split}&\sum_{i\leq 4}\bigg(\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}})\|_{L^{2}(\underline{H})}+\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}\check{\sigma}\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)\\
\lesssim&\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\beta)\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{0,\underline{u}})}+\sum_{i\leq 4}\bigg(\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}u^{i+1}G_{1,i}\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}u^{i+1}G_{2,i}\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)\\
&+\sum_{i\leq 4}\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}u^{i+1}G_{3,i}\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.\end{split}

(8.10)

Note that the highest derivatives of the scalar field in in
G_{1,i}
satisfies

\begin{split}\|\Omega u^{i+1}\nabla^{i+1}\nabla\phi\nabla\phi&\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i+1}\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega\nabla\phi\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\mathcal{S}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

The corresponding terms in
G_{2,i}
and
G_{3,i}
obey

\begin{split}\|\Omega u^{i+1}\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\nabla\phi&\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega e_{4}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega\nabla\phi\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{S}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O=\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\mathcal{S}O\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

We then proceed to bound other terms. Among the terms in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega u^{i+1}}G_{1,i}
, we first control the contributions from the fifth derivatives the Ricci coefficients. These terms are

\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi,\Omega\hat{\chi}),\quad\frac{1}{|u|}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi),\quad\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})

(8.11)

and in
\|u^{5}\cdot\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
norm they satisfy

\begin{split}\|u^{5}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi,\Omega\hat{\chi})\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi,\Omega\hat{\chi})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{O}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}},\end{split}

\begin{split}\|u^{4}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}\|_{L^{1}_{u}}\lesssim\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a\tilde{O}}{|u|}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

Here we use the improved bound for
u^{6}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)
in Proposition
7.2
, which is better than other
\nabla^{5}(\Omega\psi\hskip 1.0pt)
components. For the terms containing
(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})
, employing the bootstrap assumption (
2.40
), we get

\begin{split}\|u^{5}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})&\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\tilde{O}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\tilde{O}O\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

For
\|\Omega u^{i+1}G_{1,i}\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
, the other terms obey

\|\Omega u^{i+1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}},\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|^{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}},

\|\Omega u^{i+1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i+1\\
i_{1},i_{3}\leq i\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|^{2}}\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|}\cdot|u|^{2}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}},

\|u^{i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|^{2}}\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|}\cdot|u|^{2}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}},

\|\Omega u^{i+1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}=i+1\\
i_{1}\leq i\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+2}\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O^{2}}{|u|^{4}}\cdot|u|^{2}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}},

\|\Omega u^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|^{3}}\cdot|u|^{2}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}.

We then proceed to derive the estimates for
G_{2,i}
and
G_{3,i}
. Since all the terms of
G_{2,i}
are contained in the expression for
G_{3,i}
, hence we only need to bound the terms in
G_{3,i}
. The terms containing the
5
derivatives of the Ricci coefficients are

\Omega u^{5}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}\psi\hskip 1.0pt,\quad\Omega u^{5}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}\underline{\eta},

and they obey

\begin{split}\|\Omega u^{5}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{O}\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}},\end{split}

\begin{split}\|\Omega u^{5}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{5}\underline{\eta}\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\|u^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}5}}\nabla^{5}\underline{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|\Omega{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\psi\hskip 1.0pt}\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\tilde{O}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\tilde{O}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

The rest terms in
\|\Omega u^{i+1}(G_{2,i},G_{3,i})\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
are lower order and they satisfy

\|\Omega u^{i+1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{4}}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}},\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}},

\|\Omega u^{i+1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i+1\\
i_{1},i_{3}\leq i\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|^{2}}\cdot\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|}\cdot|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}\underline{u}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}},

\|\Omega u^{i-1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}\underline{u}O\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Back to (
8.10
), we hence prove this proposition.

∎

We then utilize the other paired equations and proceed to prove

Proposition 8.2
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.38
), (
2.39
), (
2.40
), (
2.41
), it holds

\begin{split}&\sum_{1\leq i\leq 4}\bigg(\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi,\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}(\underline{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)\\
\lesssim&\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\bigg(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\bigg).\end{split}

By further employing the
L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})
estimates proved in Section
5.1
for
\phi
, it also holds

\begin{split}&\sum_{1\leq i\leq 4}\bigg(\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}},\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}\underline{\beta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\bigg(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\bigg).\end{split}

Proof.

We first commute the renormalized null Bianchi equations with
i
angular derivatives and get

\nabla_{3}\nabla^{i}\check{\sigma}+\frac{3+i}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i}\check{\sigma}+\mbox{div }^{*}\nabla^{i}(\underline{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla\phi)=F_{1,i},

(8.12)

where
F_{1,i}
is

\begin{split}F_{1,i}=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\nabla^{i+1}(e_{3}\phi)\nabla\phi}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-1}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i+1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-1}}}{|u|}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\check{\sigma},\end{split}

\nabla_{3}\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi})-\mbox{div }\nabla^{i}(\underline{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla\phi)+\frac{3+i}{2}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi})=F_{2,i}

(8.13)

with
F_{2,i}
being

\begin{split}F_{2,i}=&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\nabla^{i+1}(e_{3}\phi)\nabla\phi}+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-1}}}{|u|}\nabla^{i}\mu+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-1}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i+1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\\
&+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-1}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi})\\
&+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-1}}}{|u|}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-1}}}{|u|^{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0})\\
&+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-1}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\frac{1}{|u|}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi}),\end{split}

\nabla_{4}\nabla^{i}(\underline{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla\phi)-\mbox{div }\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)-^{*}\nabla\nabla^{i}\check{\sigma}=F_{3,i},

(8.14)

where
F_{3,i}
satisfies

\begin{split}F_{3,i}=&\Omega^{-2}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)+\Omega^{-2}\cdot\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}}\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega\hat{\chi},\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)+\Omega^{-2}\cdot\Omega\hat{\chi}\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})\\
&+\Omega^{-2}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})+\Omega^{-2}\cdot\Omega\hat{\omega}\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0})+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\nabla^{i+1}(\nabla\phi)\nabla\phi}\\
&+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i+1\\
i_{3},i_{4}\leq i\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0})}\nabla^{i_{4}}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi,\Omega\hat{\chi},\Omega\hat{\omega})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(K,\check{\sigma})+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{-2}}}{|u|}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\\
&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}+1}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{2}+1}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt+\Omega^{-2}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{2}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt}.\end{split}

(8.15)

We then applying Proposition
6.2
for
\phi=u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}(\underline{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla\phi)
and Proposition
6.4
for
\phi=\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)
and
\phi=\nabla^{i}\check{\sigma}
with
\lambda_{1}=i+2
and add these identities together. We then obtain

\begin{split}&\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi},\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}+\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}(\underline{\beta}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla\phi})\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\|\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi},\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{0,\underline{u}})}^{2}+\|u^{2i+4}\nabla^{i}\check{\sigma}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\cdot\Omega}F_{1,i}\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}^{2}\\
&+\|u^{2i+4}\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\cdot\Omega}F_{2,i}\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{2i+4}\nabla^{i}(\underline{\beta}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla\phi}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\cdot\Omega}F_{3,i}\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.\end{split}

(8.16)

We proceed to control terms on the right. For the term involving
F_{3,i}
we have

\begin{split}&\|u^{2i+4}\nabla^{i}(\underline{\beta}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla\phi}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\cdot\Omega}F_{3,i}\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{1}_{u}L^{1}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}(\underline{\beta}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla_{3}\phi\nabla\phi})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}u^{i+2}F_{3,i}\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}.\end{split}

In
\|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega}u^{i+2}F_{3,i}\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
, we note that the borderline terms are from the first two lines of (
8.15
). These borderline terms obey

\begin{split}\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\lesssim&\|\Omega^{-1}u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\|\frac{1}{|u|\Omega}\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a\tilde{O}(\mbox{tr}\chi)\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\mathcal{R}(\beta)\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\hat{\underline{\chi}})(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi,\Omega\hat{\chi},\Omega\hat{\omega})\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{6}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\chi},\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\hat{\underline{\chi}}\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\|u\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\|u^{5}\nabla^{5}(\Omega\hat{\chi},\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\|u\Omega^{-1}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega})\mathcal{R}(\beta)\cdot\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}(\beta)\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|\Omega}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\bigg(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\bigg)\mathcal{R}(\beta)\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\bigg(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\bigg).\end{split}

Here we employ Proposition (
7.2
), Proposition (
7.5
) and Proposition (
8.1
). And to control
\|\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}}\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
, we utilize

\begin{split}(\Omega\nabla_{4})(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})_{AB}+\frac{1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})_{AB}=&\Omega^{2}(\nabla\widehat{\otimes}\underline{\eta})_{AB}-\frac{1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}(\Omega\hat{\chi})_{AB}+\Omega^{2}(\underline{\eta}\widehat{\otimes}\underline{\eta})_{AB}\\
&+\Omega^{2}\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla_{B}\phi-\frac{1}{2}\Omega^{2}g_{AB}\nabla^{C}\phi\nabla_{C}\phi.\end{split}

Applying Proposition (
4.2
), it holds

\|\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}}\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}}{|u|}\|\Omega\hat{\chi}\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\frac{\underline{u}}{|u|}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}(O+O^{2})}{|u|^{2}}\leq\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}.

Similarly, we bound the rest terms in
\|\Omega u^{i+2}F_{3,i}\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
and they obey

\begin{split}\|\Omega u^{i+2}&\nabla^{i+1}\nabla\phi\nabla\phi\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i+1}\nabla\phi\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u\Omega\nabla\phi\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\mathcal{S}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O=\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{S}O\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{-2}u^{i+2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}=i+1\\
i_{3},i_{4}\leq i\end{subarray}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0})\nabla^{i_{4}}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\chi,\Omega\hat{\chi},\Omega\hat{\omega})\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\mathcal{R}(\beta)\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big),\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{-2}u^{i+1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\Omega^{-2}|u|\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\frac{a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}O^{2}\cdot\underline{u}|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}O^{2}}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}\Omega^{2}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|u^{i+2}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(K,\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\sum_{i_{3}\leq 4}\|\Omega u^{i_{3}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}},\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\sum_{i_{2}\leq 2}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{i_{1}+i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
&+\sum_{i_{3}\leq 2}\|u^{i_{3}+2}\nabla^{i_{3}}(K,\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\sum_{i_{1}\leq 4}\|u^{i_{1}+i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|\Omega}\cdot\underline{u}+\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|^{2}}\cdot\underline{u}|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\mathcal{R}O}{|u|\Omega}+\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}},\end{split}

\begin{split}\|\Omega u^{i+2}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}+1}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{2}+1}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\frac{\underline{u}^{3}aO^{2}}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}},\end{split}

\begin{split}\|\Omega^{-1}u^{i+2}\cdot\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{2}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i_{3}}\psi\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{1}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}O^{3}}{|u|^{\frac{3}{2}}\Omega}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}.\end{split}

In the similar manner, we also control terms in
F_{2,i}
and
F_{1,i}
. In
\|\Omega u^{i+2}F_{2,i}\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
and
\|\Omega u^{i+2}F_{1,i}\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
, the top-order derivatives appear in the next four terms. The first term satisfies

\begin{split}&\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\nabla\phi\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|\Omega^{-1}u^{i+1}\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega e_{3}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u\Omega\nabla\phi\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\mathcal{S}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\mathcal{S}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}.\end{split}

The second term obeys

\begin{split}&\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i}\mu\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{i+\frac{5}{2}}\nabla^{i}\mu\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{-\frac{3}{2}}\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\underline{u}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\Omega}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}=\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\end{split}

where Proposition (
7.4
) is used. For the third term, we have

\begin{split}&\sum_{i\leq 4}\|u^{i+2}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i+1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\lesssim&\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i_{1}+i_{2}\leq 5\\
i_{1}\leq 2\end{subarray}}\|u^{i_{1}+i_{2}+2}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\cdot\bigg(\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i_{3}\leq 4}\|u^{i_{3}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{-2}\|_{L^{1}_{u}}\\
&+\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u^{i+1}\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi},\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}})\|_{L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{u}}+\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i+1}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{-2}\|_{L^{1}_{u}}\bigg)\\
\lesssim&\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O\cdot\bigg(\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|}+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\bigg)+\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\tilde{O}}{|u|}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}},\end{split}

(8.17)

where for the first inequality of (
8.17
) we use the top-order terms are of form
\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i+1}(\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}},\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})
and
\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\nabla^{i+1}\eta
. And for the fourth term, it holds

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{-1}u^{i+2}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi)\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O}{|u|\Omega}\cdot\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)\mathcal{R}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}.\end{split}

For the rest terms in
\|\Omega u^{i+2}F_{2,i}\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
and
\|\Omega u^{i+2}F_{1,i}\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}
, we have

\begin{split}\|u^{i+1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}+1}\nabla^{i_{3}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq|u|\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O^{2}}{|u|^{2}}|u|^{2}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}=\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{4}}O^{2}}{|u|}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|\Omega^{-1}u^{i+1}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i-1}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla^{A}\phi\nabla_{A}\phi,\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\Omega^{-1}|u|^{2}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{2}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}O^{2}}{|u|^{3}}|u|^{2}\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|\Omega}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}},\end{split}

\begin{split}&\|u^{i}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0})\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\\
\leq&\|u^{i+\frac{3}{2}}\sum_{i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}=i}\nabla^{i_{1}}\underline{\psi}\hskip 1.0pt^{i_{2}}\nabla^{i_{3}}(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi}-(\Omega\mbox{tr}\underline{\chi})_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{\infty}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\|u^{-\frac{3}{2}}\|_{L^{1}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{\infty}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}},\end{split}

where Proposition (
4.6
) is employed.

Gathering the above estimates, together with Proposition (
8.1
) we then obtain

\begin{split}&\sum_{i\leq 4}\bigg(\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}},\check{\sigma})\|_{L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}+\|u^{i+2}\nabla^{i}\underline{\beta}\|_{L^{2}_{u}L^{2}(S_{u,\underline{u}})}\bigg)\\
\lesssim&\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big)(1+\mathcal{R}[\beta])}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}\big(1+\frac{\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}a^{\frac{1}{4}}}{|u|^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega}\big).\end{split}

∎

Combining Propositions
8.2
,
8.1
,
8.3
, we thus conclude

\mathcal{R}\lesssim 1.

(8.18)

Substituting this bound (
8.18
) into Proposition
7.6
, we furthermore acquire

\tilde{\mathcal{O}}_{5,2}\lesssim 1.

(8.19)

This proves the hyperbolic part of Theorem
1.1
.

We further derive an additional estimate for
K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla^{A}\phi
.

Proposition 8.3
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
1.1
and the bootstrap assumptions (
2.38
), (
2.39
), (
2.40
),(
2.41
), it holds

\begin{split}\|u^{2}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla^{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S)}\lesssim&\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}.\end{split}

Proof.

Applying Proposition
4.7
and the bootstrap assumption (
2.40
), we get

\|u^{2}(K-\frac{1}{|u|^{2}}-\frac{1}{4}\nabla_{A}\phi\nabla^{A}\phi)\|_{L^{\infty}_{u}L^{2}_{\underline{u}}L^{2}(S)}\lesssim\underline{u}^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}=\underline{u}^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{\frac{3}{4}}.

∎

The above sections finish the hyperbolic estimates. Together with a standard local existence and extension argument, we have obtained a desired spacetime existence region. We now move to the elliptic part and construct the apparent horizon within this obtained existence regime.

9.
Subsolutions and Supersolutions for the MOTS

Along each fixed
\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}
, we consider a
2
-sphere
(u,\underline{u},\omega)
on it with
\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
and
u
satisfying
u=-R(\omega,\underline{u})
. Considering
R=R(\omega,\underline{u})
as a function on
\mathbb{S}^{2}
, we define

\begin{split}\mathcal{G}(R,\underline{u})&:=\Delta_{g}R+\frac{1}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}_{g}\underline{\chi}|\nabla_{g}R|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}_{g}\chi\\
&\qquad+4\Omega\underline{\omega}|\nabla_{g}R|^{2}+2\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}}(\nabla_{g}R,\nabla_{g}R)+2g(\eta,\nabla_{g}R),\end{split}

(9.1)

where
g
is the induced metric on the 2-sphere
M=\{u=-R\}
along
\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}
.

When there is no confusion, we suppress
\underline{u}
and write
R=R(\omega)
. Following
[
6
]
, along
\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}
it holds that

u=-R(\omega)\text{ is a}\begin{cases}\text{MOTS}&\text{if $\mathcal{G}(R,\underline{u})=0$ on }\mathbb{S}^{2},\\
\text{trapped surface}&\text{if $\mathcal{G}(R,\underline{u})>0$ on }\mathbb{S}^{2},\\
\text{untrapped surface}&\text{if $\mathcal{G}(R,\underline{u})<0$ on }\mathbb{S}^{2}.\end{cases}

\underline{H}_{\underline{u}}
M_{\underline{u}}(\omega)
\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
u=-R(\underline{u},\omega)
u=-1

Figure 6.

A remaining main goal of this article is to solve
R=R(\omega,\underline{u})
, which satisfies
\mathcal{G}(R,\underline{u})=0
for each
\underline{u}
. In later sections, we will also study the regularity of
R=R(\omega,\underline{u})
in terms of
\underline{u}
.

To solve for
R=R(\omega,\underline{u})
, we first construct the anisotropic sub- and super- solutions to
\mathcal{G}(R,\underline{u})=0
. With
u=-R(\omega,\underline{u})
, on
S_{u,\underline{u}}
we define
\gamma
and
\phi
via

g=R^{2}\gamma\mbox{ and }R=(\underline{u}a)^{1-\mu^{*}}e^{-\phi}.

Remark 20
.

For perturbed Christodoulou’s initial data prescribed along
\underline{u}=0
, we can define the corresponding
\phi
via letting

R\cdot\Omega(-R,0)^{2}=\underline{u}ae^{-\frac{\phi}{1-\mu^{*}}}.

Remark 21
.

Compared with
[
6
]
by An-Han, there
\mu^{*}=0
and
a\gg 1
. In this paper, we have
0<\mu^{*}<1
and we can set
a=1
. Two major observations in below elliptic arguments are that i) owing to Christodoulou’s initial data, a potentially dangersome term is with a favored sign and hence the approach to construct the multi- and single-valley anisotropic trapped surface as in
[
6
]
and
[
28
]
still works. ii) even though the borderline quadratic nonlinear term with gradient is no longer negligible as
1/a
fails to be the small parameter, the method of deriving apriori estimates via Moser’s iteration developed in
[
6
]
by An-Han is robust enough to be extended to this new setting. Besides these observations, a novel ingredient in the step of linearization is a new ansatz for the to-be-solved solution and the use of it plays a vital role.

In below, we provide the details.
Note that on
S_{u,\underline{u}}
it holds

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Delta_{g}\phi=R^{-2}\Delta_{\gamma}\phi}.

And equation
\mathcal{G}(R,\underline{u})=0
can be rewritten as

\begin{split}0=\Delta_{\gamma}\phi&-|\nabla_{\gamma}\phi|^{2}-4\Omega\underline{\omega}R|\nabla_{\gamma}\phi|^{2}+2\gamma^{ij}\eta_{i}\partial_{j}\phi+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}2R^{-1}\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}}_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\nabla_{i}\phi\nabla_{j}\phi}\\
&-\frac{R}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}_{g}\underline{\chi}|\nabla_{\gamma}\phi|^{2}+\frac{R}{2}\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}_{g}\chi.\end{split}

(9.2)

We point out that the main contribution in
\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}_{g}\chi
is

\frac{2}{R}\cdot(1-\mu^{*})-\frac{\underline{u}af(\underline{u},\omega)}{R^{2}\Omega^{2}_{0}(R)}\cdot(1-\mu^{*})

and
\Omega_{0}^{2}(R):=\Omega^{2}(-R,0)=R^{\frac{\mu^{*}}{1-\mu^{*}}}
. We then write
\frac{1}{2}R\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}_{g}\chi
as

\frac{1}{2}R\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}_{g}\chi=1-\mu^{*}-\frac{\underline{u}af(\underline{u},\omega)\cdot(1-\mu^{*})}{2R\Omega^{2}_{0}(R)}+[\frac{1}{2}R\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}_{g}\chi-(1-\mu^{*})+\frac{\underline{u}af(\underline{u},\omega)\cdot(1-\mu^{*})}{2R\Omega^{2}_{0}(R)}].

Together with the fact

R\Omega_{0}^{2}(R)=R\cdot R^{\frac{\mu^{*}}{1-\mu^{*}}}=R^{\frac{1}{1-\mu^{*}}}=[(\underline{u}a)^{1-\mu^{*}}e^{-\phi}]^{\frac{1}{1-\mu^{*}}}=\underline{u}ae^{-\frac{\phi}{1-\mu^{*}}},

(9.3)

we can rewrite
\mathcal{G}(R,\underline{u})=0
as

\begin{split}0=\Delta_{\gamma}\phi&+(1-\mu^{*})-\frac{1}{2}f(\underline{u},\omega)(1-\mu^{*})e^{\frac{\phi}{1-\mu^{*}}}-4\Omega\underline{\omega}R|\nabla_{\gamma}\phi|^{2}\\
&-(1+\frac{R}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}_{g}\underline{\chi})|\nabla_{\gamma}\phi|^{2}+2\gamma^{ij}\eta_{i}\partial_{j}\phi+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}2R^{-1}\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}}_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\nabla_{i}\phi\nabla_{j}\phi}\\
&+[\frac{1}{2}R\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}_{g}\chi-(1-\mu^{*})+\frac{1}{2}f(\underline{u},\omega)(1-\mu^{*})e^{\frac{\phi}{1-\mu^{*}}}].\end{split}

Multiplying
1/(1-\mu^{*})
on both sides of the equation and setting

\widetilde{\phi}:=\frac{\phi}{1-\mu^{*}},

we then transfer
\mathcal{G}(R,\underline{u})=0
into

\begin{split}0=S(\widetilde{\phi},\underline{u})=&\Delta_{\gamma}\widetilde{\phi}+1-\frac{1}{2}f(\underline{u},\omega)e^{\widetilde{\phi}}-{4(1-\mu^{*})\Omega\underline{\omega}R}|\nabla_{\gamma}\widetilde{\phi}|^{2}\\
&-(1+\frac{R}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}_{g}\underline{\chi})(1-\mu^{*})|\nabla_{\gamma}\widetilde{\phi}|^{2}+2\gamma^{ij}\eta_{i}\partial_{j}\widetilde{\phi}\\
&+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}2(1-\mu^{*})R^{-1}\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}}_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\nabla_{i}\widetilde{\phi}\nabla_{j}\widetilde{\phi}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+[\frac{R}{2(1-\mu^{*})}\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}_{g}\chi-1+\frac{1}{2}f(\underline{u},\omega)e^{\widetilde{\phi}}]}.\end{split}

(9.4)

And we denote

\begin{split}F(\widetilde{\phi}):=&-(1+\frac{R}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}_{g}\underline{\chi})(1-\mu^{*})|\nabla_{\gamma}\widetilde{\phi}|^{2}+2\gamma^{ij}\eta_{i}\partial_{j}\widetilde{\phi}\\
&+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}2(1-\mu^{*})R^{-1}\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}}_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\nabla_{i}\widetilde{\phi}\nabla_{j}\widetilde{\phi}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+[\frac{R}{2(1-\mu^{*})}\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}_{g}\chi-1+\frac{1}{2}f(\underline{u},\omega)e^{\widetilde{\phi}}]}.\end{split}

(9.5)

Applying
g=R^{2}\gamma
and the estimates in Section
3.1
, we have that
\gamma
satisfies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\leq\sqrt{|\gamma|(u,\omega)}\leq\frac{3}{2},\quad

\displaystyle\Lambda^{-1}I\leq\gamma_{ij}(u,\omega)\leq\Lambda I,

(9.6)

\displaystyle|D_{\omega}\gamma_{ij}(u,\omega)|+

\displaystyle R|\partial_{u}\gamma_{ij}(u,\omega)|\leq\Lambda,

(9.7)

\displaystyle|D^{2}_{\omega}\gamma_{ij}(u,\omega)|+R|D_{\omega}\partial_{u}

\displaystyle\gamma_{ij}(u,\omega)|+R^{2}|\partial^{2}_{u}\gamma_{ij}(u,\omega)|\leq\Lambda

(9.8)

with
\Lambda
being a uniform positive constant.

To proceed, we use the below lemma in
[
6
]
:

Lemma 9.1
.

(Lemma 3.5 in
[
6
]
) Let
\{\gamma(s)\}
be a family of metrics and
\{\phi(s)\}
be a family of functions, both parametrized by
s
.
Then,

(\Delta_{\gamma}\phi)^{\bm{\cdot}}=\Delta_{\gamma}\dot{\phi}-\gamma^{ij}\gamma^{kl}\dot{\gamma}_{jl}\nabla^{2}_{ik}\phi-\gamma^{ij}\gamma^{kl}\nabla_{i}\dot{\gamma}_{jl}\nabla_{k}\phi+\frac{1}{2}\gamma^{ij}\gamma^{kl}\nabla_{l}\dot{\gamma}_{ij}\nabla_{k}\phi,

(9.9)

where we denote
\dot{\,}=\frac{d\,}{ds}
.

As a corollary, via the same proof as stated in Lemma 3.6 of
[
6
]
, it holds

Lemma 9.2
.

Set
\widetilde{\phi}\in C^{2}(\mathbb{S}^{2})
to be an arbitrary positive function. Then, we have

|\Delta_{\gamma}\widetilde{\phi}-\Delta_{\gamma_{0}}\widetilde{\phi}|\leq C(1+\widetilde{\phi})(|\nabla_{\gamma}^{2}\widetilde{\phi}|+|\nabla_{\gamma}\widetilde{\phi}|+|\nabla_{\gamma}\widetilde{\phi}|^{2})e^{\widetilde{\phi}}a^{-\frac{1}{3}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{\tilde{\delta}}}

(9.10)

with
C
a positive constant and
\tilde{\delta}
a small positive constant, independently of
a
and
\widetilde{\phi}
.

Remark 22
.

Note that the additional
a^{-\frac{1}{3}}\Omega^{\tilde{\delta}}
is gained by applying

\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}}{|u|}=\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega^{2}}{|u|\Omega^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\underline{u}a^{\frac{1}{2}}\Omega^{2}}{\underline{u}ae^{-\widetilde{\phi}}}=e^{\widetilde{\phi}}a^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Omega^{2}\ll e^{\widetilde{\phi}}a^{-\frac{1}{3}}\Omega^{\tilde{\delta}}

in the proof. Here for the first inequality we use (
9.3
).
And for the last inequality, if setting
a=1
, the whole elliptic arguments would be developed in the interior region, hence we would employ the property
0<\Omega(u,0)\leq\Omega(u_{1},0)\ll 1
.

Recall that, with
R(\omega)=(\underline{u}a)^{1-\mu^{*}}e^{-(1-\mu^{*})\widetilde{\phi}}
it holds

u=-R(\omega)\text{ is a}\begin{cases}\text{MOTS}&\text{if $\mathcal{S}(\widetilde{\phi},\underline{u})=0$ on }\mathbb{S}^{2},\\
\text{trapped surface}&\text{if $\mathcal{S}(\widetilde{\phi},\underline{u})<0$ on }\mathbb{S}^{2},\\
\text{untrapped surface}&\text{if $\mathcal{S}(\widetilde{\phi},\underline{u})>0$ on }\mathbb{S}^{2}.\end{cases}

Back to (
9.4
), to construct the subsolution (untrapped surface), we just set
\underline{\widetilde{\phi}}(\omega)=\ln(\frac{3}{2})
. Note
0\leq f\leq 1
. Using (
9.5
) and hyperbolic estimates, we have

|F(\widetilde{\phi})|\leq Ce^{\widetilde{\phi}}a^{-\frac{1}{3}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{\tilde{\delta}}}(|\nabla_{\gamma}\widetilde{\phi}|^{2}+1).

For
\Omega
being sufficiently small in the interior
, it holds

S(\ln(\frac{3}{2}),\underline{u})=1-\frac{f}{2}\frac{3}{2}+F(\ln(\frac{3}{2}))\geq 1-\frac{3}{4}+F(\ln(\frac{3}{2}))>0.

(9.11)

Hence, the
2
-sphere
S_{u,\underline{u}}
with
u=-(\underline{u}a)^{1-\mu^{*}}e^{-(1-\mu^{*})\widetilde{\phi}}=(\frac{2}{3}\underline{u}a)^{1-\mu^{*}}
is an untrapped surface.

To construct the supersolution (trapped surface), we need
S(\bar{\tilde{\phi}},\underline{u})<0
. Here we apply a key property from the hyperbolic parts:

\Omega\underline{\omega}(u,\underline{u})\geq\frac{1}{2}\Omega\underline{\omega}(\underline{u},0)=\frac{\mu^{*}}{8(1-\mu^{*})}\cdot\frac{1}{r(u,0)}>0.

Thus, back to (
9.4
) to find the supersolution
\bar{\tilde{\phi}}
, it suffices to require

\begin{split}\Delta_{\gamma}\bar{\tilde{\phi}}&+1-\frac{1}{2}f(\underline{u},\omega)e^{\bar{\tilde{\phi}}}-(1+\frac{R}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}_{g}\underline{\chi})(1-\mu^{*})|\nabla_{\gamma}\bar{\tilde{\phi}}|^{2}\\
&+2\gamma^{ij}\eta_{i}\partial_{j}\bar{\tilde{\phi}}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}2(1-\mu^{*})R^{-1}\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}}_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\nabla_{i}{\bar{\tilde{\phi}}}\nabla_{j}{\bar{\tilde{\phi}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+[\frac{R}{2(1-\mu^{*})}\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}_{g}\chi-1+\frac{1}{2}f(\underline{u},\omega)e^{\bar{\tilde{\phi}}}]}<0.\end{split}

Notice that the one-valley supersolution
\bar{\tilde{\phi}}
given in Lemma 3.7 of
[
6
]
and the multi-valley supersolution
\bar{\tilde{\phi}}
constructed in Theorem 7.4 of
[
6
]
fulfill this requirement. Since
-4(1-\mu^{*})\Omega\underline{\omega}R|\nabla_{\gamma}\bar{\tilde{\phi}}|^{2}\leq 0
, with these
\bar{\tilde{\phi}}
we also have
S(\bar{\tilde{\phi}},\underline{u})<0
. As listed in Lemma 3.7 of
[
6
]
, for any
\tau>2.2
, the constructed
\bar{\tilde{\phi}}
obeys

\bar{\tilde{\phi}}_{min}=-\log(m\epsilon^{2})+C(\tau)\quad\mbox{ and }\quad\bar{\tilde{\phi}}_{max}=-\log(m\epsilon^{\tau+2})+C(\tau).

(9.12)

Adopting this
\bar{\tilde{\phi}}
, we have that the
2
-sphere
S_{u,\underline{u}}
with
u=-(\underline{u}a)^{1-\mu^{*}}e^{-(1-\mu^{*})\bar{\tilde{\phi}}}
is a trapped surface and it holds

-(\underline{u}a\cdot m\epsilon^{2})^{1-\mu^{*}}\leq u\leq-(\underline{u}a\cdot m\epsilon^{\tau+2})^{1-\mu^{*}}.

In below, for equation (
9.4
) we seek the solution
\widetilde{\phi}
bounded between
\underline{\tilde{\phi}}=\ln(\frac{3}{2})
and
\bar{\tilde{\phi}}
constructed above as in
[
6
]
. We require that
\widetilde{\phi}
satisfies

0<\ln(\frac{3}{2})\leq\widetilde{\phi}\leq\kappa

(9.13)

with
\kappa
being the maximum of the supersolution
\bar{\tilde{\phi}}
. Here
\kappa=\kappa(m,\epsilon)
depends on constants
m
and
\epsilon
stated in Theorem
1.1
. We further define constant
K
via

K:=\max\{C(\kappa),|\bar{\widetilde{\phi}}|_{C^{2}(\mathbb{S}^{2})}\}

(9.14)

with

C(\kappa)=(Ce^{\kappa})^{Ce^{4\Lambda\kappa e^{\kappa}}}

(9.15)

being explicitly given in Theorem
10.4
.

Throughout this paper, we will use parameters
a
, function
\Omega
and constant
\kappa
, which satisfy

K=\max\{(Ce^{\kappa})^{Ce^{4\Lambda\kappa}},\|\bar{\tilde{\phi}}\|_{C^{2}}\}\leq a^{\frac{1}{4}}\cdot\Omega^{-\tilde{\delta}/2}\ll a^{\frac{1}{3}}\cdot\Omega^{-\tilde{\delta}}.

(9.16)

Here,
C
is a uniform positive constant defined in Theorem
10.4
, and
\Lambda
is introduced in (
9.6
).
Note that for the interior region
, we have
a=1
and
0<\Omega\lesssim\Omega(u,0)\leq\Omega(u_{1},0)\ll 1
. When
\kappa=\kappa(m,\epsilon)
is fixed, in both regions we choose either parameter
a
being sufficiently large or
0<\Omega\leq\Omega(u,0)
being sufficiently small, such that (
9.16
) holds.

Remark 23
.

We can set
a=1
and the above arguments hold. Moreover, the hypersurface
u=-(\underline{u}a\cdot m\epsilon^{\tau+2})^{1-\mu^{*}}
locates within the hyperbolic existence region.

10.
Regularity Estimates via Moser’s Iteration

With
u=-R
and
R=(\underline{u}a)^{1-\mu^{*}}e^{-(1-\mu^{*})\widetilde{\phi}}
, via (
9.4
),
S_{-R,\underline{u}}
being a MOTS requires

\begin{split}0=&\Delta_{\gamma}\widetilde{\phi}+1-\frac{1}{2}f(\underline{u},\omega)e^{\widetilde{\phi}}-4(1-\mu^{*})R\Omega\underline{\omega}|\nabla_{\gamma}\widetilde{\phi}|^{2}-(1+\frac{R}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}_{g}\underline{\chi})(1-\mu^{*})|\nabla_{\gamma}\widetilde{\phi}|^{2}+2\gamma^{ij}\eta_{i}\partial_{j}\widetilde{\phi}\\
&+[\frac{1}{2(1-\mu^{*})}R\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}_{g}\chi-1+\frac{1}{2}f(\underline{u},\omega)e^{\widetilde{\phi}}]+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}2(1-\mu^{*})R^{-1}\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}}_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\nabla_{i}\widetilde{\phi}\nabla_{j}\widetilde{\phi}}.\end{split}

(10.1)

And we denote

\displaystyle F_{1}=

\displaystyle-4(1-\mu^{*})R\Omega\underline{\omega}|\nabla_{\gamma}\widetilde{\phi}|^{2},

(10.2)

\displaystyle F_{2}=

\displaystyle-(1+\frac{R}{2}\Omega\mbox{tr}_{g}\underline{\chi})(1-\mu^{*})|\nabla_{\gamma}\widetilde{\phi}|^{2}+2\gamma^{ij}\eta_{i}\partial_{j}\widetilde{\phi}

(10.3)

\displaystyle+[\frac{1}{2(1-\mu^{*})}R\Omega^{-1}\mbox{tr}_{g}\chi-1+\frac{1}{2}f(\underline{u},\omega)e^{\widetilde{\phi}}]+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}2(1-\mu^{*})R^{-1}\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}}_{kl}\gamma^{ik}\gamma^{jl}\nabla_{i}\widetilde{\phi}\nabla_{j}\widetilde{\phi}}.

Our aim in this section is to prove Theorem
10.4
. We take three steps. In the first step, we control the Hölder norms of the solutions via using the local boundedness and the weak Harnack inequality due to Moser. In the second step, we estimate the Hölder norms of solutions’ first derivatives. This step is established based
on an integral characterization of Hölder continuous functions.
In the third step, we bound the Hölder norms of solutions second derivatives.
During the proof, we track the dependence on
\kappa
.

With (
10.2
) and (
10.3
), the above equation (
10.1
) can be rewrite as

-\Delta_{\gamma}\tilde{\phi}=1-\frac{1}{2}fe^{\tilde{\phi}}+F_{1}+F_{2}

(10.4)

with
\widetilde{\phi}=\widetilde{\phi}(\omega)
being the solution and
0\leq f\leq 1
on
S_{u,\underline{u}}
. Note that (
10.4
) is a quasilinear elliptic equation for
\tilde{\phi}
;
\gamma
depends on
\widetilde{\phi}
;
F
contains the gradient of
\tilde{\phi}
.

Remark 24
.

For notational simplicity, we set
B_{r}(p)
to be the standard ball centered at
p
with radius
r

in
\mathbb{R}^{2}
, which lies in a coordinate chart of
\mathbb{S}^{2}
. For notational simplicity, when there is no danger of confusion, we also write
B_{r}(p)\subset\mathbb{S}^{2}
. When the center is located at the origin, we often write
B_{r}
for short. When emphasizing the radius
r
, we also employ the notation
B_{p}(r)
.

With coordinates, we can rewrite
10.4
as

-\partial_{j}\big(a_{ij}\partial_{i}\tilde{\phi}\big)=\sqrt{|\gamma|}\Big[1-\frac{1}{2}fe^{\tilde{\phi}}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}F_{1}+F_{2}}\Big].

(10.5)

By the hyperbolic part, we have

\displaystyle\Lambda^{-1}I\leq\big(a_{ij}(\omega,\tilde{\phi})\big)\leq\Lambda I,

for some positive constant
\Lambda
, and
\Lambda^{-1}\leq\sqrt{|\gamma|}(\omega,\tilde{\phi})\leq\Lambda
.

Allowing us to abuse notations slightly, for any
(\omega,\tilde{\phi},p)\in B_{1}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{n}
, applying the hyperbolic estimates, we can also write

F_{2}(\omega,\tilde{\phi},p)=a^{-\frac{1}{3}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{\tilde{\delta}}}e^{\tilde{\phi}}\big[c_{ij}p_{i}p_{j}+c_{i}p_{i}+c_{0}\big]

with
c_{ij},c_{i},c_{0}
being functions of
(\omega,\tilde{\phi})
on
B_{1}\times\mathbb{R}_{+}
.

Denoting
p_{i}:=D_{i}\tilde{\phi}
and
D\tilde{\phi}:=\nabla\tilde{\phi}
,
the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality then implies

|F_{2}|\leq Ca^{-\frac{1}{3}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Omega^{\tilde{\delta}}}e^{\tilde{\phi}}(|D\tilde{\phi}|^{2}+1)\leq c(|D\tilde{\phi}|^{2}+1)

with
c
a small positive constant.

Since
F_{1}=-4(1-\mu^{*})R\Omega\underline{\omega}|\nabla_{\gamma}\widetilde{\phi}|^{2}
and
R\Omega\underline{\omega}\geq\frac{\mu^{*}}{8(1-\mu^{*})}>0
, here we only have

|F_{1}|\leq|D\tilde{\phi}|^{2}+1.

While in
[
6
]
,
\Omega\underline{\omega}
obeys the same upper bound as for
\Omega\hat{\underline{\chi}}
and there
|F_{1}|
satisfies the same estimate
|F_{1}|\leq c(|D\widetilde{\phi}|^{2}+1)
with
c
being a small positive constant as for
|F_{2}|
here. Hence it is negligible in
[
6
]
.

In this paper,
F_{1}
is of size
1
and serves as the main term. A
key
observation of this paper (also pointed out one line below (4.3) in
[
6
]
) is that the regularity estimates carried out in
[
6
]
also work for the scenario

|F_{1}|+|F_{2}|\leq c(|D\widetilde{\phi}|^{2}+1)\quad\mbox{ with }\quad c=1.

(10.6)

In below, we start to conduct these elliptic estimates. We then write (
10.5
) in divergence form as

\int a_{ij}(\omega,\tilde{\phi})D_{i}\tilde{\phi}D_{j}\tilde{\psi}\,d\omega=\int\sqrt{|\gamma|}\Big[1-\frac{1}{2}fe^{\widetilde{\phi}}+F(\omega,\widetilde{\phi},D\widetilde{\phi})\Big]\tilde{\psi}\,d\omega,

(10.7)

with
\tilde{\psi}
being any function in
H^{1}_{0}(B_{1})\cap L^{\infty}(B_{1})
. We also assume
\tilde{\phi}=\tilde{\phi}(\omega)\in H^{1}(B_{1})
and require

0\leq\tilde{\phi}\leq\kappa\quad\text{on }B_{1}

(10.8)

with
\kappa
being some positive constant,
which is related to the
anisotropicity
via the construction of upper and lower barriers
.

Our first goal is to bound the Hölder norm of
\tilde{\phi}
via Moser’s iteration. For the estimates, we pay attention to their explicit dependence on
\kappa
. Following the steps in the proof of Theorem 4.2 in
[
6
]
, we obtain

Proposition 10.1
.

Let
\tilde{\phi}\in H^{1}(B_{1})
be a positive solution to
(
10.7
) with (
10.8
) satisfied. Then, it holds

[\tilde{\phi}]_{C^{\alpha}(B_{1/2})}\leq Ce^{\kappa}

(10.9)

with
C
being a positive constant depending only on
\Lambda
. And
\alpha
is given by

\alpha=\epsilon_{0}e^{-4\Lambda\kappa}\in(0,1).

(10.10)

Here
\epsilon_{0}>0
is a small constant depending on
\Lambda
.

Proof.

For
s\in(0,1)
, we set

M(s){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=\max_{B_{s}}{\tilde{\phi}},\quad m(s){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}}=\min_{B_{s}}{\tilde{\phi}}.

Writing
m=m(s)
and
M=M(s)
